{"forms":[{"form":"walk-ins","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A facility or room which may be walked into:","A relatively small room (such as a closet or pantry) or refrigerator or freezer that is spacious enough to walk into."]},{"glosses":["A facility or room which may be walked into:","A relatively larger room or (especially) an apartment that is entered directly, not via an intervening passage or lobby."]},{"glosses":["A facility or room which may be walked into:","A facility or an event that principally handles customers who do not have an appointment."]},{"glosses":["A facility or room which may be walked into:","A facility accessed on foot rather than by car, usually contrasted to drive-in."]},{"glosses":["Someone who walks in (to a place, etc):","A customer, job applicant or similar who visits a restaurant, medical facility, car dealership, etc. without a reservation, appointment, or referral."]},{"glosses":["Someone who walks in (to a place, etc):","A defector (or similar) who walks into an embassy (etc) unannounced."]},{"glosses":["A demonstration or protest in which the participants assemble outside a facility, gain media exposure, and enter the facility in unison."]},{"glosses":["A person whose original soul has departed the body and been replaced with another."],"topics":["parapsychology","pseudoscience"]},{"glosses":["A headmate (fictive, factive, or neither) who shows up in a system fully formed."],"qualifier":"multiplicity slang"}],"upstream_line":696461,"upstream_offset":2829029546,"upstream_record_sha256":"d4783bca696d5d6c28164fc6d6410aa4096d061342a2e8ee6dd47625be22d946","word":"walk-in"}
